# Claims

Every factual claim in a chapter is listed here with its evidence. Evidence is never written into the prose.

Statuses:

- pending: written, evidence not yet run or fetched
- verified: the test, recorded run (see `verification-log.md`) or fetched source confirms it
- cut: removed from the chapter because it could not be verified, or because the prose no longer exists (r02 domain switch)
- design (plan), realised in later chapters: a domain fact stated in the plan (`_plans/domain-model.md`) that no code or test yet shows; it is checked when the chapter that builds it ships
- UNBACKED: no evidence found; must be fixed or cut before release

Evidence is a test, a run or a source. Ref is the chapter file and line of the claim, then the test class, log entry or bibliography entry. Rewritten at r02 G1 (2026-11-10) for the order-to-delivery domain.

| 15-n | 15 | The tail is read with a Panache projection (fetch size 500) and not entities; a Panache stream() of entities puts every row in the persistence context so replaying 100,000 events holds 100,000 entities until the transaction ends; a projection to a record leaves nothing managed | spike S3 r06 (list() OOM at Xmx 128, entity stream() OOM at Xmx 96, stream() with em.detach and projection pass at 96); w15w-x-heap96.log 27/27 with projection at -Xmx96m; w15w-x-entities.log and w15-run2-entities-evidence.log (OutOfMemoryError) ; re-run after repair round 1: r1-15-x-entities96.log (precondition 3 fails), r1-15-x-entities96-evidence.log (container exit 3, 'Terminating due to java.lang.OutOfMemoryError: Java heap space'), r1-15-x-heap96.log (27/27) | _docs/15-snapshots.md:52-59 | verified |

| 15-q | 15 | A snapshot never conflicts with an appending command (own table, keyed to a version); reads never write, so a GET on a stream that has none stays slow until a command arrives | SnapshotPolicyTest (a read writes nothing; eightWritersOnOneSkuLoseNothing); verify.sh 15 checks 9, 17 (r1-15-base1..3.log) | _docs/15-snapshots.md:80 | verified (tests r1-15-mvn.log; checks r1-15-base1..3.log) |

| 15-s | 15 | SnapshotterRoute is Young's out-of-band snapshotter: a timer, a query for the streams too far past their snapshot, a split, and one call per stream; take loads through the same StockLedger and writes with the same guarded upsert in its own transaction | SnapshotterRouteTest.theSchedulerSnapshotsAStreamThatIsPastNAndCommandsDoNot; verify.sh 15 checks 19 to 24 (r1-15-base1..3.log); sources.md r06 Young (snapshotter outside the application server, queries for aggregates gone past the allowed number of events) and Camel Scheduler, SQL, Split pages; spike S4 r06 | _docs/15-snapshots.md:84-97 | verified (tests r1-15-mvn.log; checks r1-15-base1..3.log) |
